[44] 07-11 23:35 84b0c46ab699 c2665168d7c7
7月10日のコミットが怪しい。特に「Improve support for Android firefox. Issue #60」。差分を確認する。\n\nNow let me check the open issues and the build artifacts to understand the full picture.\n\nLet me check the manifest and content script to understand the root cause.\n\nNow let me check the build files commit and the content-script source to understand the full picture.\n\n調査結果をまとめます。\n\n## 調査結果\n\n### 更新内容（7月10日のコミット群）\n\nnos2x-fox v1.19.0 → v1.20.0 への更新で、主な変更は以下の通り：\n\n1. **`a03995c` - Android Firefox対応 (Issue #60)**\n   - `src/background.ts` のみ変更\n   - `browser.windows.onRemoved.addListener` を `if (browser.windows)` でガード\n   - Android Firefox向けに `browser.tabs.onRemoved` へのフォールバック追加\n   - `promptPermission` / `promptPin` 内の `browser.windows.get()` も同様にガード\n\n2. **ビルドスクリプト変更** - ファイル名にバージョン番号を含めるように変更\n\n3. **バージョンバンプ** - 1.19.0 → 1.20.0\n\n### エラーの根本原因\n\nログから判明すること：\n\n| エラー | 原因 |\n|---|---|\n| `Unable to load script: moz-extension://.../content-script.js` | nos2x-foxのcontent scriptが読み込めない |\n| `window.nostr is not defined` | content scriptが動いていないためNIP-07プロバイダ未注入 |\n| `Failed to obtain public key: Timeout` | `window.nostr`を待ってタイムアウト |\n| relay接続拒否 (nokotaro, holybea, nostr.band) | relay側の問題（nos2x-fox更新とは無関係） |\n\n**content script (`content-script.ts`) は今回のコミットで変更されていない。** manifest.json の `content_scripts` 設定も変更なし。\n\n### 推測される原因\n\nbackground.ts の `browser.windows` ガード変更は**デスクトップFirefoxでは問題にならない**（`browser.windows` は定義されているため）。したがって：\n\n- **ビルド/パッケージングの問題**の可能性が高い。v1.20.0の `.xpi` にcontent scriptが正しく含まれていないか、ビルド時に何らかの問題が発生した可能性\n- Firefoxのアドオン更新時にファイルが壊れた可能性もある\n\n### 対処法\n\n1. **拡張機能を無効化→再有効化** してみる\n2. **アンインストール→再インストール** する\n3. それでも直らない場合、[GitHub Releases](https://github.com/diegogurpegui/nos2x-fox/releases) から **v1.19.0の `.xpi` を手動ダウンロード** してインストールする\n4. [nos2x-foxのIssues](https://github.com/diegogurpegui/nos2x-fox/issues) で報告する（Android対応のbackground.ts変更後にcontent scriptが読込めなくなることがある旨）

On the contrary, it is history’s largest and most transparent digital surveillance network, bending the knee before states and corporate giants, where every single penny can be tracked. Never forget: fools pray for a transparent blockchain; the wise take refuge behind the unbreakable cryptographic wall of civil disobedience and mathematics. For the tokens of transparent ledgers -where your neck is leashed by KYC collars, your wallet is peeked into by anyone, and exchanges can taint and blacklist them at a whim- are the very embodiment of digital feudalism rather than a financial revolution. While marximalists, who still call this state-sanctioned, pseudo-libertarian simulation of slavery "money" -having long lost the property of fungibility, the most sacred rule of economics, where one unit is no longer equal to another- are trapped in a massive delusion, the true technical rebellion lives on through Monero, rising with absolute transactional privacy at the exact intersection of anarcho-capitalism, crypto-anarchism, and agorism; for financial privacy is not a refuge for criminals, but the final fortress of human dignity and individual self-defense, and the digital shackles of central banks will be rendered utterly powerless against pure mathematics operating without permission -because what you cannot track, you cannot manipulate, block, or tax; and what you cannot control, you can never corrupt or enslave.\nhttps://blossom.primal.net/4e3caeba2b43ee5c6ee17d29c42e812e52c1e53cc6c53d616b0a0a5c214527c3.png\nKripto dünyasının en büyük yalanıyla yüzleşmenin ve egemen güçlerin sistematik olarak zerk ettiği o öğrenilmiş çaresizliği mutlak bir kaosla parçalamanın vakti geldi: #Bitcoin artık bir özgürlük aracı değildir! Bilakis, devletlerin ve kurumsal devlerin önünde diz çöken, her kuruşunun izlenebildiği tarihin en büyük ve en şeffaf dijital gözetleme ağıdır. Unutmayın ki ahmaklar şeffaf bir blokzincir için dua eder, bilgeler ise sivil itaatsizliğin ve matematiğin kırılmaz şifreleme duvarına sığınır. Çünkü KYC tasmalarıyla boynunuzun bağlandığı, cüzdanınızın herkes tarafından dikizlenebildiği ve borsaların keyfine göre "kirli" ilan edip kara listeye alabildiği şeffaf defterlerin tokenleri bir finansal devrimden ziyade, dijital feodalizmin ta kendisidir. İktisat biliminin en kutsal kuralı olan birimdenklik (fungibility) becerisini çoktan kaybetmiş, bir birimin diğeriyle eşit olmadığı, devlet onaylı bu psödö-özgürlükçü kölelik simülasyonuna hâlâ "para" diyen marksimalistler büyük bir yanılgı içindeyken, gerçek teknik başkaldırı anarko-kapitalizm, kripto-anarşizm ve agorizmin tam kesişim noktasında, mutlak mübadele mahremiyetiyle yükselen #Monero ile yaşamaya devam ediyor; zira finansal mahremiyet suçluların sığınağı değil, insan onurunun ve bireysel özsavunmanın son kalesidir ve izin istemeden çalışan saf matematik karşısında merkez bankalarının dijital prangaları hükümsüz kalacaktır, çünkü izleyemediğin şeyi manipüle ve bloke edemez, vergilendiremezsin, kontrol edemediğin şeyi asla yozlaştıramaz, köleleştiremezsin.
---

[155] 07-11 17:22 85bdb5875e11 6621f24ea64c
The Bitcoin Policy Institute just filed to intervene as a defendant in a New York lawsuit that should concern every bitcoiner who self-custodies.\n\nA pseudonymous plaintiff "Noah Doe" is trying to use New York's 1958 lost-and-found property law to claim ownership of ~39,069 dormant bitcoin addresses holding an estimated 3.8 million BTC (~$293B).\n\nThe argument is if your bitcoin hasn't moved in 5+ years, anyone can download your public address, report it to the NYPD as "found property," and claim it's theirs.\n\nThe addresses targeted include wallets linked to the Mt. Gox hack, the Counterparty burn address, and 21,000+ addresses attributed to Satoshi Nakamoto.\n\nMatthew Boyer is stepping in because they hold bitcoin long-term, just like millions of other HODLers, and this legal theory would criminalize the most basic principle of self-custody, holding.\n\nInactivity is not abandonment. A public address is not "found property." And a law written for lost wallets and umbrellas has no business being applied to the Bitcoin blockchain.\n\nA hearing is set for July 14. The outcome could set major precedent for property rights in bitcoin.\nhttps://blossom.primal.net/07e4add4b88ee5f8075ebc8192935c95f073323ca75c9dada1aaa3afff5ccc65.jpg

[209] 07-11 15:40 1ec454734dcb 502ef7c3f408
I've been holding off on writing my own browser, but I suppose it is time.\nhttps://blossom.laantungir.net/608ca12f2424a59c8f13582cd5c1df295787435be7b752caec38d08659ea07d2.jpg\nI mean, why not have 20 different projects in various stages of completion, instead of finishing one at a time?\n\n"They all work together" I say to myself.\n\n# sovereign_browser\n\nA Linux x86 web browser built in C99 on WebKitGTK, designed around **sovereign identity** instead of the traditional web's permissioned infrastructure.\n\nThe thesis: the browser's security model (DNS/domains, TLS/CAs, same-origin policy, CORS, cookie sandboxing) is what forces you to rely on permissioned domains, certificate authorities, and centralized account systems. By deprecating that model and replacing it with **Nostr identity** and **FIPS\nmesh transport**, you get a browser that's more capable, not less safe — because trust moves to the layer where it belongs: your keys.
